## 📌 Project Overview
- **Project:** MapIt — location-first buy-and-sell marketplace for India (and USA)
- **Stack:** Node.js + Express (Vercel serverless) · Supabase (DB + Auth + Storage) · Resend (SMTP) · Leaflet.js (maps) · Single-file vanilla JS frontend
- **Root directory:** `/Users/nageshnagarajarao/Documents/Mapit project/mapit-backend` *(MacBook Air — migrated from Windows 2026-06-08)*
- **Last updated:** 2026-07-23, checkpoint (**Session 8 item 4 (Reported-chat visibility) — UAT-PASSED by Nagesh, committed.** Design check-in held first (per Rule 1): 4 decisions confirmed with Nagesh — (1) hide the "View Chat" option entirely when no conversation exists for a report, rather than showing a dead-end note; (2) log every admin view (new `report_chat_views` table: report_id, admin_id, viewed_at) since this is the single most sensitive thing an admin can view in the app — real private chat content between two users; (3) show real nicknames, not generic "Buyer"/"Seller" labels, since Arun needs to know who's who to judge a report fairly; (4) a separate modal/popup, matching the existing Review Listings/Feedback pattern rather than an inline expand. **Backend**: `GET /api/listings/reports/queue` (`src/routes/listings.js`) now batch-checks which reports have a matching conversation (reporter as buyer, on the reported listing) via one extra query — no N+1 — and returns `has_conversation` per report. New `GET /api/listings/reports/:reportId/chat` (`requireAuth`+`requireAdmin`) resolves the conversation, returns the full message thread with sender nicknames, and logs the view to `report_chat_views`. 404s if no conversation is found. **Frontend** (`MapIt_Admin_v1.html`/`public/admin.html`): a "💬 View Chat" button appears on a report row only when `has_conversation` is true; opens a read-only modal (`chatviewOverlay`) with each message's nickname, role tag, timestamp, and content — includes an explicit "this view is logged" note in the modal itself. **Real bug found + fixed during Nagesh's first UAT pass**: the button never appeared at all, even for reports that genuinely had a matching conversation — root-caused (verified directly against the dev DB with disposable test data, not guessed) to Nagesh's local server being a plain `node src/server.js` process (no nodemon auto-reload) that had been running since before the `has_conversation` backend code was added, so it was still serving the old route logic. Not a code bug — fixed by killing the stale process (PID 10725) and confirming a fresh `npm start` picks up the change. **After the restart, Nagesh click-tested and confirmed all 3 scenarios**: button appears for a reported listing where he (as Test4) had chatted with the seller first; modal opens and shows the correct nickname/role/timestamp/content for each message; button correctly stays hidden for a listing reported without any prior chat. **Verified in code**: `node -c` clean, script parse-check clean, both two-file diffs clean (admin.html and index.html), server boots clean, both routes correctly 401 without a token, and a full disposable-data DB test (insert test report → confirm has_conversation true/false correctly → resolve conversation → fetch messages with nicknames → log the view → clean up) passed end-to-end before Nagesh's own UAT pass. Session 8 item 4 is now closed.)

- **Last updated (previous):** 2026-07-20, ~14:00, checkpoint (**Session 8 item 1 committed (`c1b8bba`); item 2 (Suspend/Reinstate real enforcement) — CODE COMPLETE, not yet UAT-tested.** Design decision made and documented before building (per Rule 1): enforce `suspended` inside `requireAuth` itself (checked on every authenticated request), not just at sign-in — a login-only check would have left an already-signed-in suspended user with full access until their token happened to expire, which defeats the point of an admin hitting Suspend mid-incident. Trade-off accepted: one extra `profiles` read per authenticated request app-wide, negligible at current family/friends-beta scale, flagged (not solved) as a revisit-if-it-scales item, same treatment as other scale-later notes already in this file. Fail-open on a missing profile row, since `POST /api/auth/register` (itself `requireAuth`-gated) runs before a brand-new user's `profiles` row exists — confirmed by reading the actual `signup`/`register` code, not assumed. `src/middleware/auth.js` needed `supabaseAdmin` imported (previously only used the anon-key `createClient` for JWT validation); server boots clean with the new import (no circular-dependency issue). The three admin list routes (`GET /pending/all`, `GET /reports/queue`, `GET /users/feedback/all`) now also return each seller/submitter's live `suspended` status via their existing joins, so the admin page can show real state without extra round-trips. **Admin page**: the old one-way "⛔ Suspend Seller" button (Reported Listings only) replaced with a shared `suspendBtnHtml()`/`toggleUserSuspension()` pair used consistently across all three sections (Pending, Reported, Feedback) — button flips label/color in place after a successful toggle rather than re-fetching the list; confirm-dialog copy differs by direction (suspend warns "will prevent signing in," reinstate reassures "restores access immediately"). **Main app**: the shared `api()` helper now special-cases a `403` with `suspended:true` — instead of letting it surface as a random generic "Error: ..." toast wherever it happens to fire, it forces a clean sign-out (`forceSuspendedSignOut()`, guarded against double-firing from concurrent in-flight requests) with one clear dedicated message, then reloads to the sign-in screen. **Verified so far**: all touched files parse/syntax-check clean, both two-file diffs clean, server boots and the unauthenticated-path regression checks (401 with no/bad token on `/api/auth/me`, `/admin` and `/` still 200) are unchanged. **Not yet verified** (needs a real Supabase session/test account, unavailable in this environment): the actual suspend→403-on-next-request→reinstate→access-restored round trip, and a live click-through of the toggle on all three admin surfaces plus the forced-sign-out flow on a real second test account — **flagged for Nagesh to test next**. Not committed yet.)
- **Last updated (previous):** 2026-07-20, ~13:00, checkpoint (**Session 8 item 1 — `/admin` page + real admin-role gate + modal migration — UAT-PASSED by Nagesh on `localhost:3001`, committing now.** Two rounds of live bugs found and fixed during this UAT pass, both CSS specificity/completeness issues in the new `MapIt_Admin_v1.html`, not the auth logic: (1) no base `.hide{display:none}` rule existed (only a scoped `.adm-section.hide`), so the deny/loading/dashboard blocks were all visible from page load regardless of the real check result; (2) after adding `.hide`, it was declared *before* `.adm-loading`'s own `display:flex` rule — equal specificity, so the later rule won and the loading screen stayed visible forever even though DevTools confirmed the `hide` class WAS correctly applied and `is_admin:true` came back correctly. Diagnosed via a live DevTools walkthrough with Nagesh (Console — no errors; Network — `/api/auth/me` and the follow-on `pending/all` fetch both completed; `document.getElementById('admLoading').className` confirmed `hide` was present) — this ruled out the auth/JS logic and pointed straight at CSS cascade order. Fixed by moving `.hide` to the end of the stylesheet with `!important`, so it always wins regardless of what's added to the page later. Also added a 10s client-side timeout on the access check so a genuinely hung request can never leave this screen stuck indefinitely again. **Design polish, same UAT session**: replaced the pill-style tab bar (Pending/Reported/Feedback) with three clickable orange "block" cards showing a live item count each, per Nagesh's reference mockup — same `switchAdmSection()` logic underneath, all three counts now warm in the background as soon as the dashboard loads. **New Session 8 item 10 logged** (see 🔜 Next Steps): a real metrics dashboard (total users, active users, region-wise pie chart) — explicitly deferred, not built now, keeps item 1 scoped to page+gate+migration+polish only. Nagesh confirmed the full flow works end-to-end and the new block design looks good. About to commit.)

**ToS/Privacy Policy finalization — LLM Council reviewed, drafted to v0.3 thin, still not published (2026-07-16 → 2026-07-18).** Active thread, most recent work:
- **LLM Council convened (2026-07-16)** on "should MapIt incorporate before/around MVP launch, given the drafted ToS?" — 5 advisors (Contrarian, First Principles, Expansionist, Outsider, Executor), each independently answered, then peer-reviewed each other's anonymized responses. **Verdict:** near-unanimous (4/5 advisors, and every one of 5 peer-review passes independently rated the Contrarian's response strongest) — the incorporation question was a red herring. The real defect: the draft ToS's ₹5,000 liability cap and indemnification clause only make legal sense if a real corporate entity stands behind them; MapIt as a sole proprietorship doesn't have one, so those clauses were legally hollow (a natural person can't indemnify himself against his own negligence via an unnegotiated document). Recommendation: don't incorporate this week (prior brief's 5 triggers — money/escrow, Nagesh's equity, outside investment, hiring, scale — still stand, none have fired), but don't publish the draft ToS as-is either — fix the self-protective language and de-personalize the Grievance Officer contact first. Sole dissenting advisor (Expansionist) argued for incorporating now for fundraising/escrow optionality; every peer review flagged this as answering a hypothetical question instead of the one on the table. Full report: `docs/council-report-20260716-215934.html`; full transcript: `docs/council-transcript-20260716-215934.md`.
- **Nagesh+Arun's decision (2026-07-18):** go with the thin-ToS approach, aligned with the council's First Principles Thinker recommendation ("incorporate for the document, or de-fang the document — don't incorporate for the business"). Chose to de-fang: strip the corporate-personhood-assuming language rather than incorporate.
- **New `docs/terms-privacy-draft2.html` (v0.3, thin) created**, changes from v0.2:
  - Entity name placeholder filled in as **"Arun BN, proprietor, trading as MapIt"** (Terms §3 intro, §13 IP, Privacy §2) — no incorporation.
  - Grievance Officer contact (Terms §19, Privacy §15) **de-personalized**: `contactus@mapit.co.in` only, personal Gmail and personal phone number both removed. Confirmed this is legally sufficient — MapIt is a general intermediary under **Rule 3(2)** of the IT Rules, 2021 (name + contact mechanism, email satisfies this), not a "significant social media intermediary" under **Rule 4** (5M+ registered users, which requires a 24/7 phone-based mechanism) — a threshold MapIt is nowhere near.
  - Terms **§16 (Limitation of Liability)**: ₹5,000 cap removed, replaced with a shorter statement tied to §3's "neutral platform" framing, plus a callout explaining why (the cap only made sense with a corporate entity behind it).
  - Terms **§17**: renamed "User Responsibility for Listings & Conduct" — full indemnification legalese replaced with a plain statement that each user is responsible for their own listings/conduct and for complying with applicable law (RERA, Motor Vehicles Act, etc.), per Nagesh's explicit choice after confirming the council had flagged indemnification (not just the liability cap) as the same structural defect.
  - Privacy **§4 (How We Use Information)**: new bullet explicitly tying IP address collection to its purpose (fraud/abuse detection + IT Rules 180-day compliance logging, cross-referencing §9) — closes a DPDP purpose-limitation gap Nagesh asked about directly ("are we storing IP addresses, what legalities does this bring?").
  - Registered office address filled in: **#115, 4th Main, Arekere Mico Layout 2nd Stage, Bannerghatta Road, Bangalore 560076** (Terms §19, Privacy §2, Privacy §15). **Flagged as an open review item, not silently accepted**: this reads as a residential address about to be published in a public legal document alongside the Grievance Officer's name — same category of exposure that was deliberately avoided for the phone/personal email, just via a different channel. Rule 3(2) requires a published address (unlike the phone number, this one isn't avoidable the same way), but a virtual-office/CA-address/co-working-space alternative was suggested for Arun to consider before this goes live. Callout added directly in the draft so it surfaces when Nagesh+Arun review together.
  - Top notice box rewritten to summarize what's decided vs. still open (DPDP Consent Manager finalization, RERA registration question, arbitration-vs-courts choice — all still flagged, unchanged from v0.2).
- **Not yet done**: Karnataka-advocate review (both documents are explicitly marked "not legal advice, not ready to publish"); final decision on the residential-address flag; publishing either version live.

**Session 6 (Real-Time Chat) — DB foundation complete, chat UI queued as its own session (2026-07-16).** Scope was explicitly re-negotiated at the start of this session: the Anthropic key is being held deliberately (wait-and-see, a few months post-launch), so AI content moderation is deferred; chat infrastructure is being built now regardless, since it's valuable standalone.
- ✅ `conversations` + `chat_messages` tables created (`003-chat-schema.sql` Steps 2–3, run by Nagesh in Supabase SQL Editor), columns verified against the migration file via `information_schema.columns`.
- ✅ 5 RLS policies written and verified against `pg_policies`: `conversations` SELECT (buyer or seller) + INSERT (must be the named buyer, `buyer_id <> seller_id`, `seller_id` cross-checked against the listing's real `seller_id`); `chat_messages` SELECT + INSERT (participant of the parent conversation, via subquery) + UPDATE (marking `read_at`, restricted to non-senders). Unlike every other table in the app, these RLS policies are genuinely load-bearing, not just defense-in-depth — Supabase Realtime requires the browser to subscribe directly with the user's own session, bypassing the backend's service-role path entirely. Known accepted gap (flagged, not fixed): the UPDATE policy can't stop a recipient from also rewriting `content` while marking a message read (would need a trigger).
- ✅ Supabase Realtime enabled on `chat_messages` only (not `conversations` — no push-update need there), verified via `pg_publication_tables`.
- **Not yet done**: renaming `messages`→`messages_legacy` (deliberately deferred — the old table still powers the live "I'm Interested" flow, will break production if renamed early); the actual chat UI (frontend thread/composer/Realtime subscription, backend routes for creating/listing conversations) — explicitly agreed to be its own focused session, not started.

**RLS policy full audit — ✅ DONE (2026-07-12).** Connected directly to the production Postgres DB (read-only `pg_policies`/`information_schema` queries via a scratch Node+`pg` script, `SUPABASE_DB_PASSWORD` from `.env`) to get ground truth, since RLS was set up manually in the Supabase SQL editor and isn't tracked in `database/migrations/`. Findings:
- **Context established first**: RLS is a defense-in-depth layer, not the app's primary access control — every backend route uses `supabaseAdmin` (service-role, bypasses RLS), and the frontend never calls Supabase directly (no anon-key usage anywhere in `MapIt_MVP_v1.html`). Real enforcement is `src/middleware/auth.js` + per-route ownership checks.
- `listings`, `user_pins`, `messages` — ✅ correctly scoped, no changes needed. Verified (via a disposable table in a `BEGIN…ROLLBACK` transaction, nothing persisted) that the `listings`/`user_pins` UPDATE policies defining only `WITH CHECK` (no explicit `USING`) are safe — Postgres mirrors `WITH CHECK` into `USING` when omitted, so a row can't be "stolen" by reassigning `seller_id`/`user_id` to yourself.
- **Finding 1 (fixed)**: `profiles` SELECT policy was `USING(true)` for any authenticated user — exposed every user's `phone`, `home_lat`, `home_lng`, `home_address` to any other logged-in user. No product feature needs this (Seller Public Profile is out of MVP scope). Fixed: dropped the policy, replaced with `"Users can read own profile"` scoped to `auth.uid() = id`.
- **Finding 2 (fixed)**: `invite_codes` SELECT policy was `USING(true)` for role `public` — no login required at all, exposed `created_for` (real names) to anyone. Table is archived (`REQUIRE_INVITE_CODE=false`, Rule 9) and all 4 code paths that touch it already use `supabaseAdmin`. Fixed: dropped the policy entirely (table now default-denies SELECT for `anon`/`authenticated`, service-role unaffected).
- **Applying the fix took 3 rounds**: Nagesh's first two paste-in-one-block attempts silently no-op'd (Supabase SQL editor didn't surface an error, but the policies were unchanged when independently re-verified against the DB) — resolved by running each `DROP`/`CREATE POLICY` statement one at a time and verifying after each. Final state independently confirmed via the same scratch DB script — both fixes are live.

**POI proximity on listing post — ✅ DONE (2026-07-12).** Fire-and-forget lookup on `POST /api/listings`: after a listing is inserted and the response sent, `lookupAndStoreNearbyPois(listing.id, lat, lng)` runs unawaited (`.catch()`-logged, matches the exact `scoreAndNotify()` pattern in `messages.js`). Finds the nearest **named** school, mall, and supermarket within 2km via OpenStreetMap's Overpass API and stores `[{type, name, distance_m}, ...]` into `listings.details.nearby_pois`, merged (not overwritten) with the existing `details` JSONB so category-specific dynamic fields (bedrooms etc.) survive.
- **New files**: `src/utils/poiLookup.js` (Overpass fetch + fail-open, 8s timeout via `AbortController`), `src/utils/geo.js` (lifted `haversineM()` out of `listings.js` so both files share one implementation instead of duplicating it).
- **Real bug found + fixed during testing**: Overpass's public instance (`overpass-api.de`) returns `406 Not Acceptable` on Node's default fetch headers — required adding explicit `Accept: application/json` + a descriptive `User-Agent`. Found by testing the raw query standalone before wiring it into the route, not discovered after the fact.
- **Confirmed via live testing (not just code review)**: also hit occasional Overpass rate-limit responses (a 200-status body that isn't valid JSON) when firing requests back-to-back during testing — confirmed this is caught cleanly by the existing fail-open `try/catch` (logs, resolves to no POI data) rather than crashing anything. This is the exact risk flagged before building (Vercel serverless functions aren't guaranteed to keep a fire-and-forget promise alive after the response is sent, and Overpass is slower/less reliable than Anthropic/Resend) — accepted as consistent with the already-proven email/lead-scoring pattern; a backfill sweep for listings that never got POI data is deferred to UAT if it turns out to matter in practice, not built now (Nagesh's explicit call).
- **End-to-end verified against the real DB** (not mocked): inserted a disposable test listing at Koramangala coordinates directly via `supabaseAdmin`, ran the real `lookupAndStoreNearbyPois()`, confirmed all 3 POI types resolved with correct distances *and* that a pre-existing `details.foo` key survived the merge — then deleted the test listing. Second test at a remote rural coordinate confirmed the no-POIs-found case correctly leaves `details` untouched (frontend hides the section entirely when `nearby_pois` is absent/empty).
- **Frontend** (`MapIt_MVP_v1.html`, ~line 2219): new "📍 Nearby" block in the listing detail view, between the specs grid and description — renders `l.details.nearby_pois` with a per-type icon (🏫/🏬/🛒) and formatted distance (`450m` / `1.2km`), hidden entirely when empty. New CSS (`.ld-nearby*`) matches the existing `.ld-desc`/`.ld-specs-grid` card styling.
- Two-file sync verified clean (`diff` no output); all `<script>` blocks parse-checked via `new Function()`.
- No new env var needed — Overpass requires no API key.

**Same-location pin popup — ✅ DONE (2026-07-12).** When ≥2 listings share the exact same GPS coordinate (apartment complex, bazaar), the map now renders one stacked pin instead of fully-overlapping individual markers.
- **Grouping** (`drawMarkers()`): listings bucketed by a `lat.toFixed(6),lng.toFixed(6)` key (~11cm precision, absorbs float noise while still requiring "identical" coordinates per spec) before rendering. Groups of 1 render exactly as before (unchanged code path); groups of ≥2 render a single marker via new `mkStackIcon()` — same diamond-pin styling as `mkIcon()` plus a small orange count badge, and the price label replaced with "N listings here".
- **Click** → `openStackPopup(group, isFav)`: a Leaflet popup (`L.popup().openOn(map)`, not a per-marker `bindPopup`) with a scrollable mini-list (`.stack-popup-list{max-height:220px;overflow-y:auto}`) — each row shows emoji + title + price, clicking one calls the existing `selListing()` and closes the popup.
- **Hover** → new `showStackHoverCard(group)` reuses the existing floating `#hoverCard` element with generic "📍 N listings here" content instead of a single listing's price/specs.
- **Selection-highlight bug avoided during design**: `selListing()` already had a loop repainting every marker's icon via `mMarkers[k].setIcon(mkIcon(...))` on every selection change. Putting all of a stack's listing IDs into that same `mMarkers` map (pointing at one shared marker object) would have caused each ID's iteration to stomp the previous one's icon with the wrong data and the wrong icon function. Fixed by keeping stack markers in a **separate** array (`mStackMarkers`, `{ids, group, marker}`) untouched by the `mMarkers` loop, with `selListing()` given its own one-line loop to repaint stack icons correctly.
- **Tested**: the grouping algorithm was extracted and unit-tested standalone in Node (4 synthetic listings incl. one with sub-metre float noise) — confirmed 3 co-located listings group correctly and 1 standalone listing doesn't. No browser available in this environment (consistent with prior sessions) — all `<script>` blocks parse-checked via `new Function()`, two-file sync verified clean.
- **✅ UAT-PASSED (2026-07-12)**: Nagesh click-tested the exact regression scenario (post 2–3 test listings at identical coords → confirm badge/count → click an unrelated listing elsewhere → confirm the stack pin still shows the correct badge/count, not silently reverted to a plain single-listing icon) — confirmed correct badge and correct count held after clicking around.

**GPS confirmation preview map in post form — ✅ DONE, code complete (2026-07-12).** Read-only 180px Leaflet map added under the manual-GPS-entry fields (`#manualListingLocGroup`) — live-updates a pin as the seller types into `#manualListingCoords`, so a typo (swapped lat/lng, missing comma, stray digit) is visible before submitting instead of silently creating a listing at the wrong location.
- **Reused an existing scaffold**: `postPickerMap`/`postPickerMarker` globals were already declared (unused) near `initMap()` — apparently stubbed in during earlier planning for this exact feature. Renamed to `_postPickerMap`/`_postPickerMarker` to match the underscore-prefixed convention used by the other feature-scoped maps (`_homeLocMap`, `_addPinMap`) and built the real implementation.
- **Non-interactive by design**: dragging/scroll-zoom/double-click-zoom/box-zoom/keyboard/tap all disabled on this map instance — it's a confirmation preview only, not a picker (picking already happens via the coordinate text field or the saved-pin dropdown).
- **Validation logic matches `submitListing()`'s acceptance criteria exactly** (same `split(',')` + `parseFloat` + NaN check), plus an added lat/lng range sanity check (`±90`/`±180`) that `submitListing()` itself doesn't currently have — flagged as a minor pre-existing gap, not fixed here since it's outside this item's scope.
- **Init/teardown mirrors the established `_homeLocMap` pattern**: lazy `L.map()` init inside a `setTimeout(...,120)` (container needs to be visible/laid-out first), `invalidateSize()` after init, full teardown (`.remove()` + null out) on `closePostModal()` and whenever the dropdown is switched away from manual entry — avoids leaking a stale Leaflet instance across repeated modal opens.
- **Tested**: coordinate-parsing/validation logic extracted and unit-tested standalone in Node against 9 cases (valid pair, missing comma, non-numeric, out-of-range lat/lng, negative coordinates) — all passed. No browser in this environment to click-test the live pin rendering — **needs real testing from Nagesh** (type valid coords → pin appears; type garbage → pin disappears with a warning hint; switch away and back to manual mode → map re-initializes cleanly).
- Two-file sync verified clean; all `<script>` blocks parse-checked.

**GPS confirmation preview map — SUPERSEDED same day (see items 4/5 rebuild below).** Nagesh click-tested it, then asked (via Arun) to go further: give sellers the same tap-to-drop-pin flow used in new-user registration instead of typed GPS coordinates. The read-only preview map above was replaced, not kept.

**Seller manual-location entry rebuilt as tap-to-pin + search (2026-07-12).** Replaced raw GPS-coordinate typing with the same pattern as `initHomeLocMap`/`initAddPinMap`: a Photon-based search box (pans/zooms only, never places the pin) + an interactive tap-to-drop-pin map (draggable marker, Nominatim reverse-geocode). New `_placePostLocPin()`/`_onPostLocSearchInput()`/`_resolvePostLocAddr()` in `MapIt_MVP_v1.html`; `submitListing()` now reads `_postPickerCoords` instead of parsing a text field. Dropdown label changed `"Enter GPS coordinates manually…"` → `"Select location manually…"`.

**Follow-up (2026-07-12, Arun+Nagesh): GPS-coordinate text entry restored alongside tap-to-pin.** Many sellers get exact coordinates via a Google Maps long-press — removing typed-coordinate entry entirely was a real regression. Added a "paste GPS coordinates" input (with the original Google Maps tip) to **both** the home-location picker (registration) and the post-listing picker — search, tap-to-drop, and pasted coordinates all now converge on the same shared pin/marker state (`_placeHomeLocPin()`/`_placePostLocPin()` shared helpers). Nagesh tested and confirmed.

Nagesh confirmed it looks better.

**Session 5 items 5–10 — ALL DONE (2026-07-12), Session 5 now 100% complete.**

5. **Pin tooltip on first tap (Tester-7 scan UX) — ✅ DONE.** Desktop unchanged (click still opens detail immediately — hover already previews it). On touch devices (`_isTouchDevice()`), first tap on a single-listing pin shows the existing `#hoverCard` tooltip pinned in place with a new "View full details →" button (previously static hint text, `pointer-events:none` — now `tap-mode` class enables `pointer-events:auto`); second tap on the same pin, or the button, opens the full panel. Tapping a *different* pin swaps the tooltip immediately (matches desktop hover behavior, Nagesh's explicit call); tapping empty map or `drawMarkers()` re-running dismisses it. Stacked (co-located) pins untouched — already tap-friendly via `openStackPopup()`. Nagesh confirmed working on-device via the uat preview.
6. **Default map/list view preference (Tester-3a) — ✅ DONE.** Discovered mid-build that the toggle referenced in the original spec didn't actually exist — only dead `.vtog`/`.vbtn` CSS scaffolding was left over from an earlier design pass; the app has always shown sidebar+map together with no single-pane mode. Built for real, **mobile-only** (Nagesh/Arun's explicit scope call — desktop keeps today's always-both-panes layout untouched): a floating "☰ List / 🗺 Map" toggle visible only below 768px width, hides/shows `#sb`/`#mapc` via `#main.view-list`/`.view-map` classes. New `PUT /api/auth/default-view` persists the choice to `profiles.default_view` (column + CHECK already live since Session 2); `initApp()` applies the saved value on load (`setViewMode(mode, persist=false)`). Switching back to map mode calls `map.invalidateSize()` since Leaflet sizes itself at init time and the container may have started out hidden. Nagesh confirmed on-device.
7. **Lightweight Help modal (Tester-4c) — ✅ DONE.** Pure frontend, no server route (per original spec). Deviated from the literal spec (a standalone `?` header icon) — put "❓ Help" as the first item in the existing profile/avatar dropdown menu instead, since the header was already fairly packed and that menu already serves as the utility drawer (Change Nickname, My Locations, Inbox, etc.). Modal (reuses `.feedback-modal`/`.feedback-box` styling) covers Browse, Post, Save, Contact a seller, Delete a listing, Change your location. Pushed straight to `main` on Nagesh's explicit go-ahead (skipped `uat`, low-risk pure frontend) — `uat` was then fast-forwarded to match so the branches didn't drift.
8. **Orphaned photo cleanup (spec item 10) — ✅ DONE.** Investigated before building: `listing_photos.listing_id` already has `ON DELETE CASCADE` (verified with a disposable insert/delete test against the live DB) — the DB-row side was never the problem. The real gap is Storage: Postgres cascades never touch Supabase Storage objects. Confirmed **3 real orphaned files already sitting in production** via a live bucket scan before writing any code. Fix forward: `DELETE /api/listings/:id` (`listings.js`) now also removes the listing's Storage objects (reads `storage_path`s before deleting the row, since cascade removes the DB rows). Backfill sweep: new admin-only `POST /api/uploads/cleanup-orphaned` walks the whole bucket (`{user_id}/{listing_id}/{filename}`) and deletes anything with no matching `listing_photos` row — gated the same way as the existing admin routes (`requireAuth` only; the "admin" boundary is frontend-only today, a pre-existing gap not introduced here and out of scope to fix). Wired to a "🧹 Clean Up Orphaned Photos" button in the existing Admin modal, with a confirm dialog. Dry-run verified against production before shipping (exactly 3 known orphans flagged, zero false positives) — the real delete was left for Nagesh to trigger, which he did and got "Deleted 3 orphaned photo file(s)."
9. **Structured feedback form upgrade (spec item 11, Tester-4d) — ✅ DONE.** Discovered the "category" half already existed (`feedback.type`: suggestion/bug/complaint/praise, rendered as buttons not a dropdown) — only the rating was missing. New migration `database/migrations/005-feedback-rating.sql` (nullable `rating SMALLINT`, CHECK 1–5 — optional since not every feedback type needs a star rating, e.g. a bug report) — **run by Nagesh in Supabase, confirmed via live schema check.** Backend validates 1–5 if present. Frontend: 5-star clickable picker (optional, with a "clear" link) in the feedback modal; rating now renders as `★★★☆☆` next to the category tag in the admin "View Feedbacks" list for faster triage (the actual point of the feature) — Nagesh confirmed stars render correctly.
10. **Supabase Auth email template branding (spec item 12) — ✅ DONE.** Dashboard-only task, no code. Only two templates actually fire in this app (confirmed via code read): **Magic Link** (used for email OTP sign-in, not Confirm-Signup — the app always sets `email_confirm:true` so that template never triggers) and **Reset Password**. Provided exact subject+HTML copy (MapIt 📍 branding, `#F06030` orange, using the required `{{ .Token }}`/`{{ .ConfirmationURL }}` variables) for Nagesh to paste into Supabase Dashboard → Auth → Email Templates. Nagesh tested both — correct branded emails received.

**Session 04C item 2 (home-location text-search box) — BUILT, ITERATED, AND LIVE ON PRODUCTION (2026-07-11).** Iteration history in one place:
1. Initial build: search box on `step-homeloc`, Nominatim forward geocoding, dropdown overlay, `flyTo`-only (never auto-places pin), 400ms debounce, `countrycodes=in`.
2. **Bug found + fixed**: dropdown showed nothing for any query — Nominatim's `jsonv2` format names the classification field `category`, not `class`; the filter was checking the wrong field so every result was silently dropped.
3. Per Nagesh's feedback, tuned result scope twice: India-wide → locality+city+landmarks → **locality/city only** (final; landmarks removed since "users may get confused that only those locations are available").
4. Added a descriptive caption under the search box (Nagesh: existing tap-hint didn't explain what the *search* box does).
5. Added city-scoping: first tried Nominatim `viewbox` + `bounded=0` (soft preference) — **did not work**, confirmed via live query testing that a well-known area name still returned results from other Indian states. Switched to `bounded=1` (hard filter) on a ~40km box around the map's current center — confirmed working.
6. **Root cause found for "results only appear with correct spelling"**: Nominatim's `/search` endpoint requires a complete (or near-complete) word — "Banash" returns 0 results, only "Banashankari" (fully typed) works. It isn't built for autocomplete-as-you-type.
7. **Switched geocoding provider to Photon** (photon.komoot.io — free, no API key, built on the same OSM data, purpose-built for prefix/autocomplete search). Confirmed via live testing: "Ban" → Bengaluru results, "Bana" → Banaswadi/Banashankari, "Banash" → Banashankari variants, all correctly scoped to the city via Photon's `bbox` param. Response normalized into the same `{lat, lon, display_name}` shape so downstream rendering/pick logic needed no changes.
8. Added "start typing at least 4 letters" note + matching enforcement in `_onHomeLocSearchInput()` (copy now matches actual behavior).

**Password strength rules — BUILT AND LIVE ON PRODUCTION (2026-07-11).** Added mid-session per Nagesh's request (8+ chars, 1 uppercase, 1 special character):
- **Backend** (`src/routes/auth.js`): new `isStrongPassword()` helper enforced on `POST /signup` (new-account registration) and `PUT /password` (reset/change password). `POST /signin` deliberately untouched so existing accounts with older/weaker passwords aren't locked out — confirmed via code read that `/signup` and `/signin` are separate endpoints called from different frontend paths.
- **Frontend**: matching `isStrongPasswordFE()`; live color-coded hint text under the password field in both the register form and the reset-password step; live "✓ Passwords match" / "Passwords do not match" indicator under both confirm-password fields; "Create Account" button disabled until *all* register fields are valid (email, strong password, matching confirm, full name, nickname) with asterisks + an "All fields are mandatory" note added to the form; "Update Password" button on the reset step similarly gated on strength + match.

~~Session 04C item 2 (home-location text-search box) — DESIGN PRESENTED, AWAITING NAGESH'S DECISION (2026-07-09).~~ (superseded — see above; kept for history) Proposed reusing Nominatim forward geocoding (same free/no-key service already used for reverse geocoding on pin-drop; the paid alternatives — Geoapify/Google Places — are out of scope per Rule 11, and a curated Bangalore-areas database is the already-flagged stretch goal for later). Design: search only pans/zooms the map via `flyTo` — it never auto-places the pin, so the user still taps to confirm exactly like today; suggestions render as an absolutely-positioned dropdown so they don't reflow `step-homeloc`'s already-tight layout; 400ms debounce + `countrycodes=in`, same policy as the old (Session-4-removed) header search; fully additive and fails open if the search API errors — tap-to-drop-pin keeps working regardless. **Nagesh asked to pause and think it over — nothing built yet, no code changes for item 2.**

**Session 04C item 1 (duplicate Name/Nickname signup fields) — DONE, ITERATED TO FINAL FORM (2026-07-09).** Root cause: the register (email+password) form on `step-auth` collects Full Name + Nickname, but `step-profile` then re-displayed the exact same two fields (pre-filled) alongside Phone + ToS — asked twice in one signup flow. OTP and Google OAuth paths were never affected (they only ever see these fields once, on `step-profile`, since it's their only source for that data). Fix: `step-profile`'s `#profileNameRow` is now hidden entirely on the register path (values already captured in `ST._pendingFullName`/`_pendingNickname` and still submitted via `saveProfile()` reading the same input IDs); OTP/Google paths are unaffected and still show it. Iterated through three follow-up rounds with Nagesh: (a) tried a 2-column layout for the Name/Nickname pair reusing the existing `.form-row` grid from the Session 3 post-form redesign, (b) that surfaced a scrollbar on the register tab specifically — fixed by extending the `step-homeloc` branding-hide pattern from Session 04B to also hide `modalLogo`/`modalSub` while the register tab is active (new shared `_updateModalBrand()` helper, called from both `goToStep()` and `switchAuthTab()`), (c) after testing, Nagesh decided the 2-column Name/Nickname layout wasn't needed after all and asked to revert to stacked rows — done, while keeping the actual "don't ask twice" fix and the scrollbar fix intact. Also explicitly considered and **rejected**: merging Phone+ToS into a row (ToS consent text reads worse cramped into a half column), widening the modal (doesn't help — on phones the modal is already constrained by the viewport, not its own `max-width`, so widening the cap gains nothing since the squeeze is vertical, not horizontal), and label-beside-input for every field (bigger inconsistent redesign, cramps inputs on a narrow mobile modal, for a space win already achieved more cheaply by the branding-hide fix).

- ✅ **Chat UI bug fix — inbox navigation to seller's own listing (2026-07-19):** clicking a conversation in the Inbox for a listing the current user *sells* (not buys) incorrectly stayed on/reset to the Browse tab and leaked the listing into the Browse pin array. Root cause: `selListing()`'s tab-reset logic combined with `switchTab('mine')`'s internal `renderMyAds()` call being unawaited — that fetch could resolve *after* `openChat()` had already rendered the chat panel, silently overwriting it back to the plain My Ads list. Fixed by having `openConversationFromInbox()` set tab state directly (bypassing `switchTab()`), load `ST.myListings` itself, call `drawMarkers()`, and only ever call `renderSidebar()` once `ST.selId` is already set (inside `selListing`) — so the racy list-view branch can never fire mid-flight.
- ✅ **Chat UI fix — My Ads tab map pins + count badge (2026-07-19):** `drawMarkers()` was showing leftover Browse-tab radius/category-filtered pins on the My Ads tab (pre-existing behavior, unrelated to this session's other work, but surfaced during the bug above) — now shows only `ST.myListings` (matches the sidebar exactly, same pattern as Saved). Added a `mine-badge` count badge next to "📋 My Ads", kept in sync via `updateMineBadge()`/`loadMyAdsCount()` everywhere `ST.myListings` changes.
- ✅ **Report Listing — code complete, backend E2E-verified, Nagesh click-tested (2026-07-19):** 🚩 flag icon in listing detail (hidden on own listings — backend also blocks self-reports), reason-chip modal (Fake/Wrong price/Spam/Offensive/Other) + optional note, `POST /api/listings/:id/report` in `src/routes/listings.js`. One report per `(listing_id, reporter_id)` via `UNIQUE` constraint + `upsert` — re-reporting updates the existing row instead of duplicating, confirmed via direct DB check. New `database/migrations/006-listing-reports.sql` (RLS enabled, insert/update-own policies, deliberately no SELECT policy — admin-only via service-role client, same "default-deny, backend reads only" pattern as the audit_log design). - ✅ **Session 5 item 3 — same-location pin popup, DONE + UAT-PASSED (2026-07-12):** Co-located listings (identical GPS, 6dp match) now render as one badged stacked pin instead of fully-overlapping markers; click opens a scrollable Leaflet popup mini-list, hover shows a generic "N listings here" card. **Design trap caught and fixed before shipping**: `selListing()`'s existing per-marker icon-repaint loop (keyed by `mMarkers[id]`) would have silently corrupted a stacked pin's badge/icon the first time *any other* listing was clicked, if all of a stack's listing IDs pointed at the same shared marker object in that same table — fixed by keeping stack markers in a separate `mStackMarkers` array with their own repaint logic. Grouping algorithm unit-tested standalone in Node. **Nagesh click-tested the exact regression scenario** (stack a pin → click elsewhere → confirm badge/count still correct) and confirmed pass.
- ✅ **Session 04C item 2 — home-location text search box, built + shipped to production (2026-07-11, commit `107b8c8`):** Search box on `step-homeloc` pans/zooms the map to a typed area via `flyTo` — never auto-places the pin, tap-to-confirm unchanged. Switched provider from Nominatim to **Photon** (photon.komoot.io, free/no-key) after confirming via live testing that Nominatim's `/search` can't autocomplete on partial words (needs a complete word). Results hard-restricted to ~40km around the map's current city via Photon's `bbox` (Nominatim's `bounded=0` soft bias was tested and confirmed ineffective — cross-state results still appeared). Filtered to locality/city-level only (`osm_key` in `place`/`boundary`) — landmarks explicitly excluded per Nagesh's feedback. Fixed an early bug where the dropdown never showed anything (Nominatim's `jsonv2` field is `category`, not `class` — root-caused via a live curl test). Added a descriptive caption and a "type at least 4 letters" note with matching enforcement.

Previously the trailing slash caused every comparison to fail, silently falling back to production for the UAT preview URL and any other trusted origin. Security check itself unchanged (Rule 8 preserved) — only the comparison input is normalized.

Map initializes on `openPinsModal()` and tears down on `closePinsModal()` (same leak-avoidance pattern as the home-location map). `renderPinsList()` now omits the Remove button entirely when only one pin exists, and disables it (new `.pin-action-btn:disabled` CSS + explanatory tooltip) for the active pin. Confirmed via code review that backend `DELETE /api/pins/:id` already promotes another pin to default gracefully when the default is removed, so this is a frontend-only UX guardrail — no backend change made.

- ✅ **Session 5 #2 — Claude-based spam/lead-qualifying check built, locally verified, and committed (2026-07-06, branch `feat/session-05-lead-scoring`, commit `cd74c99`):**
  - **Design decisions locked in with Nagesh** (all via explicit confirmation): check runs passively server-side, no separate Q&A gate; whatever the verdict, the seller email **always sends** (never silently dropped/blocked), only flagged `[Unscreened]` when not confirmed genuine; scoring criteria universal (not per-category); new additive-only migration approved.
  - **Blocking discovery**: today's "I'm Interested" tap sent identical fixed boilerplate text (`Hi! I'm interested in your listing "X". Please get in touch.`) — no buyer-authored content existed for any classifier to read. Resolved by making the note editable in place (small inline textarea, not a modal/separate gate) instead of a single-tap fixed send.
  - **New `src/utils/leadScoring.js`**: `scoreLead(noteText)` calls Anthropic's Messages API directly via raw `fetch` (no SDK, matches `email.js`'s lean-dependency pattern), model `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`, `max_tokens:5` (one-word answer only). Buyer text passed as user-turn content wrapped in `<buyer_note>`, never concatenated into the system prompt — basic prompt-injection hygiene, since the model's output only ever narrows to a hard-validated enum (`spam`/`genuine`/`unscreened`), never trusted or interpolated directly. 4s timeout via `AbortController`. Never throws — missing key, network error, timeout, non-2xx, or unexpected output all resolve to `unscreened`.
  - **`src/routes/messages.js`**: new `scoreAndNotify(message, listing, buyerId, messageContent)` helper — awaits `scoreLead()`, persists the verdict with a single `await`-ed `.update()` inside try/catch (explicitly not a chained `.catch()` on the Supabase builder — the exact anti-pattern that caused the `64cfdff` bug), then calls `notifySellerOfInterest()`. That function gained a 4th `verdict` param: `genuine` path is byte-identical to before; any other verdict prepends an orange warning banner + `[Unscreened] ` subject prefix. Call site swapped one line: `notifySellerOfInterest(...).catch(...)` → `scoreAndNotify(...).catch(...)`, same fire-and-forget shape.
  - **New migration `database/migrations/004-lead-scoring.sql`**: `ALTER TABLE messages ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lead_verdict TEXT` + `lead_scored_at TIMESTAMPTZ` — additive, nullable, no `CHECK` constraint (enum already enforced in app code per Rule 10). **Already run by Nagesh in Supabase** — confirmed via REST query.
  - **`.env.example`**: new `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` entry added, matching the `RESEND_API_KEY` 2-line comment style.
  - **Frontend (`MapIt_MVP_v1.html`)**: `expressInterest()` split into `openInterestNote()` / `cancelInterestNote()` / `sendInterestNote()`. New `#interestBox` sibling `<div>` (inside `.ld-info-bar`, below `.ld-info-top` — avoids fighting the fixed-width 3-button `.ld-info-actions` row) with a `<textarea maxlength="500">` + Cancel/Send buttons, shown/hidden via this file's existing per-id `.hide` convention.
  - **UX refinement (post-review with Nagesh)**: textarea starts empty with the old boilerplate as a `placeholder` hint (not a pre-filled `value`) so every sent message is buyer-authored; Send button starts `disabled` and only enables once the textarea has non-empty content (`oninput="updateInterestSendState()"`). Rationale discussed: doesn't stop a scripted bot hitting the API directly, but raises the bar against low-effort drive-by taps and guarantees the classifier always has real content to work with.
  - Two-File sync done — `public/index.html` diff clean.
  - **Local CORS bug found + fixed (unrelated to this feature)**: local `.env` had a stale `APP_URL=http://localhost:3000` left over from an old dev setup, but the server actually runs on `PORT=3001` and serves the frontend itself via `express.static` — so the browser's `Origin: http://localhost:3001` wasn't in the CORS allowlist, blocking local login entirely. Fixed by correcting `APP_URL` to `http://localhost:3001` in the local (never-committed) `.env`.
  - **End-to-end verified locally** (no `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` set yet): sent two real test messages via the new inline note box; confirmed via direct Supabase REST query that both rows got `lead_verdict='unscreened'` + `lead_scored_at` populated; Nagesh confirmed the seller notification email arrived with the `[Unscreened]` subject prefix and warning banner. This also re-validates the missing-key fail-open path (tested standalone via a throwaway script: missing key → `unscreened` with a console warning, never throws; invalid key → Anthropic 401 → `unscreened`, never throws).
  - **Deliberately not yet done**: `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` creation — Nagesh wants Arun's financial sign-off first (new recurring cost, however small, per Rule 11). Feature is fully safe to leave in this state — everything fails open to `unscreened`, nothing is blocked or silently dropped.

- ✅ **Bug found + fixed — `.catch()` chained on Supabase query builder crashed first-message sends (2026-07-06, commit `64cfdff`, pushed to `uat`):** Manual retest of the seller-notification flow on the deployed `uat` preview returned the error toast `Could not send: supabaseAdmin.from(...).select(...).eq(...).single(...).catch is not a function`. Root cause: `src/routes/messages.js` (pre-existing code from commit `96642c9`, unrelated to Session 5) chained `.catch(() => ({data:null}))` directly onto a `.single()` Postgrest query builder inside the inquiry-count-increment block, which only runs on a buyer's **first** message on a listing — i.e. every "I'm Interested" tap. The installed `supabase-js`/`postgrest-js` builder only implements `.then()`, not `.catch()`, so the chained call threw a `TypeError` that propagated up and failed the *entire* `POST /api/messages` request before the message was even inserted — meaning first-time "I'm Interested" sends have likely been silently broken in production this whole time, not just something introduced by the new email feature. **Fix:** wrapped the two Postgrest calls in a plain `try/catch` block instead of chaining `.catch()` on the builder (`src/routes/messages.js:168-182`); confirmed no other file in the codebase has the same anti-pattern (`grep -rn "\.catch(" src/`). Verified `node -c`, re-pushed to `uat`, Nagesh re-tested and confirmed the fix — message sent successfully and notification email received.
- ✅ **Session 5 #1 seller-notification verified end-to-end on deployed `uat` preview (2026-07-06):** After the hotfix above, Nagesh re-ran the "I'm Interested" test on the `uat` Vercel preview (`https://mapit-backend-git-uat-nagesh-n-arun.vercel.app`) — confirmed the message sent successfully and the seller-notification email arrived and was readable in the inbox. This is the first verification against the actual deployed environment (the 2026-07-02 test was local/dev only).
- ✅ **Session 5 #1 — merged to `uat` (2026-07-06):** Nagesh confirmed `RESEND_API_KEY` added to Vercel (Production + Preview scopes). Verified `origin/uat` was a direct ancestor of `feat/session-05-seller-notification` (clean fast-forward, no conflicts possible) before touching branches. Working tree had ~20 unrelated pre-existing uncommitted/untracked files (settings, skills, docs, session logs) not part of this feature — stashed with `git stash push -u` before switching branches, restored via `git stash pop` immediately after, to avoid mixing unrelated in-progress work into the merge. Merged `feat/session-05-seller-notification` (`df714d8`) → `uat` via `git merge --ff-only` (4 files: `.env.example`, `Context.md`, `src/routes/messages.js`, `src/utils/email.js`), pushed to `origin/uat`. Session 5 item #1 is now code-complete, live-send-verified, and live on the `uat` deployment.
- ✅ **Session 5 #1 — Seller notification email on "I'm Interested" (2026-07-02, branch `feat/session-05-seller-notification`, commit `df714d8`):**
  - New `src/utils/email.js` — `sendEmail({to,subject,html})` posts directly to the Resend REST API via native `fetch` (Node 18+, no SDK dependency added); `escapeHtml()` helper for interpolating buyer-supplied text into HTML email bodies safely
  - `src/routes/messages.js` — `notifySellerOfInterest(listing, buyerId, messageContent)` added; fires only on a buyer's **first** message per listing (reuses the existing inquiry-count `count === 0` check) so it won't spam the seller once Session 6 real-time chat exists; looks up seller email via `supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.getUserById()` (same admin-API pattern already used in `auth.js`) and buyer nickname via `profiles`
  - Called fire-and-forget (not awaited) after the message insert succeeds — a Resend outage or missing key can never fail the message send or the API response; errors are caught and logged without PII
  - `.env.example` — `RESEND_API_KEY` uncommented (was documented but inactive)
  - **Resend API key setup (2026-07-02):** the pre-existing `mapit-supabase` Resend key (used by Supabase's own SMTP integration for OTP/password-reset emails) could not be reused — Resend only ever shows a key's value once, at creation, and that one was created a month prior with no saved copy. Nagesh created a **new, separate key** (`mapit-backend`, "Sending access" only) — deliberate choice for blast-radius isolation: if this key is ever rotated/revoked, Supabase's auth emails keep working untouched, and vice versa. Added to local `.env` (value never shared in chat, only existence-checked via `grep -c`)
  - **Live send test (2026-07-02):** ran a one-off diagnostic script (scratchpad, not committed) that exercised the real `supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.getUserById()` + `sendEmail()` path against real Supabase data (a real UAT listing "MTB bike for fitness freaks", seller = Nagesh, buyer nickname = Arun) — Resend accepted the send, and **Nagesh confirmed the email was received** in `nagesh.aadi@gmail.com`. Feature is functionally proven end-to-end
  - Verified: `node -c` on both files, local server boots clean, `POST /api/messages` still correctly 401s unauthenticated requests — no regression to existing message-send flow
  - **Still not done (see 🎯 Current Goal for the 2 remaining steps):** `RESEND_API_KEY` not yet added to Vercel (Production + Preview); branch not yet merged to `uat`

- ✅ **Arun Session 2 UAT failures fixed (2026-06-29, commit `7919558`, pushed to `uat`):**
  - **A9 — Home location showed "Koramangala" instead of saved address:** Root cause: `step-homeloc` saved to `profiles.home_*` only; `initApp()` reads `user_pins` for the header badge; new users had no pins so the hardcoded HTML default "Koramangala" in `activePinLabel` was never replaced. Fix: `saveHomeLocation()` now also calls `POST /api/pins` with the saved coordinates + `is_default:true`; backend already handles unsetting other defaults.
  - **C4 — Password reset email linked to production (www.mapit.co.in) not UAT:** Root cause: Supabase redirect URL allowlist entry `https://mapit-backend-*.vercel.app/**` requires a path (`/**` glob); bare `window.location.origin` has no path so Supabase silently dropped `redirect_to` and fell back to Site URL. Fix: `sendPasswordReset()` now sends `window.location.origin + '/'` (trailing slash satisfies `/**`); Nagesh also added bare-origin entry `https://mapit-backend-*.vercel.app` in Supabase Dashboard → Auth → Redirect URLs. Supabase email template confirmed correct (`{{ .ConfirmationURL }}`).
  - **I3 — Sign-in page flashed briefly during Google OAuth new-user flow:** Root cause: `authModal` had no `hide` class in the HTML — it was visible from first render with `step-auth` as the active step; the ~100–300ms between HTML render and JS execution caused the flash. Fix: added `hide` to `authModal` in HTML; all code paths that need to show it already call `classList.remove('hide')` explicitly.

- ✅ **MVP Session 2 — Rate limit fix (2026-06-21, commit `b1cdc56`, pushed to `uat`):**
  - **Root cause 1 (global limiter)**: `app.set('trust proxy', 1)` was missing — Vercel edge IP was used for rate limiting so ALL users shared one 100-req bucket, exhausted quickly during testing; now uses real client IP from `X-Forwarded-For`
  - **Root cause 2 (auth limiter)**: `authLimiter` (max 5/hr, named `OTP_RATE_LIMIT`) was applied to ALL `/api/auth` routes — signup + me + register = 3 calls per test cycle, 2 cycles → blocked; it should only guard OTP routes
  - **Fix**: `app.set('trust proxy', 1)` added before rate limiter setup in `server.js`; global limit raised 100→200 (per real IP); `authLimiter` removed from `/api/auth` bulk route
  - **Fix**: `otpLimiter` (10/hr, same `OTP_RATE_LIMIT` env var) added directly to `/send-otp` and `/verify-otp` in `auth.js` — only OTP endpoints are now rate-limited strictly

- ✅ **MVP Session 2 — "MapIt User" root cause fixed in submitAuth() (2026-06-21, commit `3d100ab`):**
  - **Root cause**: Old OTP-flow accounts already had a profile row (`full_name = nickname = 'MapIt User'`) so `isNewUser = false`; `step-profile` was skipped entirely on email+password sign-in → names never updated in DB
  - **Fix**: Added `needsProfile` flag in `submitAuth()` that also routes to `step-profile` when `data.profile.nickname` is missing or equals `'MapIt User'`; pre-fills from `ST._pendingFullName`/`ST._pendingNickname` or falls back to existing (non-placeholder) profile value
  - **DB join analysis**: `invite_code_legacy` was NULL for all profiles; `invite_codes` has no email column; joined via `auth.users` (SELECT p.id, u.email FROM profiles JOIN auth.users ON u.id=p.id) — Nagesh manually updated correct rows with real names, deleted remaining "MapIt User" rows
  - **localStorage diagnosis**: Clearing Chrome cookies/cache does NOT clear localStorage; stale "MapIt User" buttons in "Who's using MapIt?" were from `mapit_sessions` localStorage; fix: run `localStorage.clear()` in DevTools console, reload

- ✅ **MVP Session 2 — "Who's using MapIt?" nickname display fix (2026-06-21, commits `b1cdc56` + `55b9859`):**
  - **Root cause**: Old `mapit_sessions` localStorage entries had `name: 'Mapit User'` and `nickname: ''` from pre-MVP code; `renderWhoamiScreen()` falls through nickname → created_for → name → shows 'Mapit User'
  - **Fix 1 (`b1cdc56`)**: `continueAs()` now falls back to `data.profile?.full_name` when `data.profile?.nickname` is null — existing accounts without nickname set will get their full_name after first click-through
  - **Fix 2 (`55b9859`)**: `_refreshStaleSessionNames()` added — on `init()`, background-fetches `/api/auth/me` in parallel for every stored session with no nickname, updates localStorage cache, re-renders list automatically within ~1 second (no click required)
  - **Fix 3 (Supabase SQL, 2026-06-21)**: Migration 001 re-run; `nickname`, `home_lat`, `home_lng`, `home_address`, `agreed_tos_at` columns confirmed MISSING from profiles table and added; `UPDATE profiles SET nickname = full_name WHERE nickname IS NULL` backfilled all existing users

- ✅ **MVP Session 2 — Google OAuth `redirectTo` fix (2026-06-20, commits `99baa73` + `cddfcb6`, pushed to `uat`):**
  - **Root cause 1**: `uat.mapit.co.in` points to Production branch in Vercel (free tier limitation — branch-specific custom domains require Pro); new code only on Vercel preview URL
  - **Root cause 2**: `signInWithGoogle()` used `hostname.includes('uat')` to decide `?uat=1`; Vercel preview URL hostname (`mapit-backend-git-uat-nagesh-n-arun.vercel.app`) doesn't contain "uat" → `?uat=1` never sent → backend hardcoded `redirectTo: https://www.mapit.co.in` → Google came back to production (old code) → invite code screen
  - **Root cause 3**: Supabase Redirect URLs allowlist lacked `uat.mapit.co.in` and Vercel preview pattern → Supabase silently dropped any non-allowlisted `redirectTo` and fell back to Site URL (`www.mapit.co.in`)
  - **Fix (frontend)**: `signInWithGoogle()` now sends `?redirectTo=<encoded window.location.origin>` — works for any hostname
  - **Fix (backend)**: `GET /api/auth/google` validates `redirectTo` against `ALLOWED_OAUTH_ORIGINS` array + `/^https:\/\/mapit-backend-[a-z0-9-]+\.vercel\.app$/` regex; falls back to `www.mapit.co.in` if invalid
  - **Fix (Supabase — pending)**: user must add `https://mapit-backend-*.vercel.app/**` to Supabase → Auth → URL Configuration → Redirect URLs (4 entries already present; adding 1 more)
  - `public/index.html` synced from `MapIt_MVP_v1.html` — diff clean

- ✅ **Session 13 — UAT Phase 4: family-testing bug reports (commit `3150cfa` on `uat`, pushed 2026-06-06):**
  - **Search dropdown**: typing in the search bar now shows a clickable type-ahead dropdown (`#srchDD`) instead of immediately filtering the left panel; only clicking a result opens that listing — other matches are then labelled "🔍 Other listings matching '...'" (not "nearby", since search results may be geographically far)
  - **Search match count**: right-justified, light-colored count of total matches shown next to results
  - **"Posted X ago" relative time**: new `timeAgo(dateStr)` util (`just now` / `Xm ago` / `X.Xhr ago` / `X.Xd ago`); wired into listing cards (`lr-meta`) and detail view (`ld-dist-sub`)
  - **Fixed My Ads cross-contamination bug**: other sellers' listings were appearing inside My Ads (with a working-looking "✏️ Edit Listing" button). Root causes: (1) `renderSidebar`/`selListing` computed prev/next nav and merges from `ST.listings` (the browse array, which explicitly excludes the user's own listings) instead of a dedicated My Ads array, and prev/next nav called `selListing()` without `keepTab=true`, silently flipping back to the browse tab; (2) `switchTab()` never cleared `ST.selId`, so a listing opened via map-pin click while on Browse stayed "selected" after switching to My Ads, skipping `renderMyAds()` and rendering that listing's detail under the `ST.tab==='mine'` context — which unconditionally showed the Edit button. Fixed by adding `ST.myListings` (populated in `renderMyAds`), making `renderSidebar`/`selListing` tab-aware end to end, clearing `ST.selId` in `switchTab`, and gating the Edit button on `l.seller_id===ST.user?.id`
  - `public/index.html` re-synced and verified byte-identical to `MapIt_Demo 30052026.html`

- ✅ **Session 17 — Schema analysis: `profiles` ↔ `invite_codes` join (2026-06-16):**
  - Join key confirmed: `invite_codes.used_by = profiles.id` (populated when a user registers with a code)
  - **Blocker**: `invite_codes.used_by` is NULL for all rows — no family member has fully registered via code yet, or `used_by` was never backfilled
  - SQL provided: `UPDATE profiles SET full_name = ic.created_for FROM invite_codes ic WHERE profiles.id = ic.used_by` (will work once `used_by` is populated)
  - Alternative approaches identified: join via `auth.users.email` if `invite_codes` has email column; or manual row-by-row mapping using a helper SELECT with row numbers

- ✅ **Session 16 — Invite code MAPIT-F-12 whitespace fix (2026-06-15, Supabase SQL Editor):**
  - **Problem**: family member reported "Invalid or expired invite code" for `MAPIT-F-12`; other codes worked fine
  - **Root cause**: NOT RLS — `validate-invite` endpoint uses `supabaseAdmin` (service role) which bypasses RLS entirely; error path `!invite` means row not found; leading/trailing space in `code` column from manual table-editor insert caused `.eq('code', 'MAPIT-F-12')` to miss the row
  - **Fix**: `UPDATE invite_codes SET code = trim(code) WHERE code LIKE '%F-12%'` removed the whitespace; verified all rows now have exactly 10-char codes (`SELECT code, length(code), created_for FROM invite_codes ORDER BY created_at DESC`)
  - **Preventive**: run `UPDATE invite_codes SET code = trim(code) WHERE code != trim(code)` after any future manual batch insert

- ✅ **Session 1** — Vercel backend setup, Supabase DB/Auth/Storage, Resend SMTP configured, CORS wired
- ✅ **Session 2** — `app.listen()` guarded with `require.main === module` for Vercel serverless; `API_URL` updated to `https://api.mapit.co.in` in both HTML files; DNS confirmed propagated (`api.mapit.co.in` CNAME → Vercel)
- ✅ **Session 3** — 6 fixes to `MapIt_Demo 30052026.html`: title, API_URL toggle comment, onboarding copy, resolved filter bug, "Mark as Resolved" visibility, Back button always visible
- ✅ **Session 3** — Fixed Resend sender domain to `noreply@mapit.co.in`; OTP root cause diagnosed (Supabase was using resend.dev test domain)
- ✅ **Session 5 — Name display & admin menu fixes (all implemented):**
  - `created_for` (invite-code name) now takes priority over `profile.full_name` everywhere; root fix for "Mapit User" display
  - WhoAmI screen now shows actual family member name (`u.created_for || u.name`) and correct initials
  - `continueAs()` passes stored invite code to `/api/auth/me` as fallback so `created_for` resolves even when `used_by` isn't set in DB
  - `continueAs()` immediately hides admin items before API call (prevents bleed from previous admin session); always writes `mapit_invite` to localStorage
  - `logout()` now explicitly hides admin menu items before page reload
  - Backend `/api/auth/me` accepts optional `?invite_code=` param and falls back to code-based lookup
  - Backend `/feedback/all` now JOINs `invite_codes` to return `created_for` per feedback; feedback viewer shows actual names
  - Session store updated: `created_for` saved as separate field; refreshed on every successful `continueAs`
- ✅ **Session 4 — 10 UX/logic fixes (all implemented):**
  - Admin menu (Review Listings / View Feedbacks) now only visible for MAPIT-N-01 and MAPIT-A-01 (localStorage fallback for invite code added)
  - Pending listings review: subcategory-correct icon (🛵 for 2-Wheelers etc.) + person's name from `seller.full_name`
  - GPS badge in header + map now auto-center to registered location after pin added/changed (`loadAndRenderPins` syncs `ST.activePin`, header label, user marker, and `map.setView`)
  - **Multi-user session switch**: `mapit_sessions` localStorage store holds all family sessions; WhoAmI screen is now a family-picker list; switching to a known user restores session instantly (no OTP); graceful fallback to OTP on session expiry
  - Pin count ("X pins visible") moved from top-right (behind zoom buttons) to bottom-center pill
  - GPS badge in header no longer navigates to My Locations (click removed); My Locations stays only in profile menu
  - My Locations "Set Active": confirm dialog before activating, toast confirmation, auto-close modal
  - Share Feedback button disabled + spinner on click to prevent duplicate submissions
  - Welcome splash now per-user (`mapit_welcomed_${userId}`) — each family member sees onboarding only at first-ever registration
  - **Inbox** added to profile menu: loads `/api/messages/inbox`, shows admin replies with sender, preview, time, unread orange dot + badge count
- ✅ **Session 7 — Full go-live (Phases 1–5 complete):**
  - Imported 6 historical session entries into `session-log.html` (Sessions 1–6); entries renumbered; total 9 entries
  - Reverted `API_URL` from `localhost:3001` → `https://api.mapit.co.in` in `MapIt_Demo 30052026.html` (line 678)
  - Synced `public/index.html` with `MapIt_Demo 30052026.html` — was 150 lines short (missing all Sessions 4-6 fixes)
  - Committed Sessions 4-6 changes as `96642c9`; synced `public/index.html` as `98fa40f`; pushed to `github.com/NageshVani/mapit-backend`
  - Diagnosed Vercel frontend `FUNCTION_INVOCATION_FAILED`: root `vercel.json` always read regardless of Root Directory setting, routing everything to Express
  - Fixed by serving frontend from Express itself: added `express.static(path.join(__dirname, '../public'))` to `src/server.js`
  - Disabled Helmet CSP (`contentSecurityPolicy: false`) to allow inline scripts and external CDN resources (Leaflet, Font Awesome, Google Fonts)
  - Added `mapit.co.in` + `www.mapit.co.in` as domains on the backend Vercel project (commit `f6200ae`)
  - Phase 3 (Supabase OTP + Auth URLs) confirmed complete by user; OTP template already had `{{ .Token }}`
  - **Phase 5 smoke test passed**: auth flow, map UI, admin menu gating, inbox badge — all verified live at `https://mapit.co.in`
  - Final live check: `mapit.co.in` → 308→www, `www.mapit.co.in` → 200 HTML, both API endpoints → 200
- ✅ **Session 6 — UX fixes (all implemented):**
  - **Admin menu visibility root fix**: Added missing CSS rule `.uav-item.hide{display:none;}` — without it, `classList.toggle('hide')` had zero visual effect; Review Listings + View Feedbacks were always visible to ALL users regardless of invite code
  - **Invite code screen skipped for returning users**: `continueAs()` catch block now pre-fills invite code and email from stored session, calls `goToStep('step-email')` — expired-session re-auth only requires OTP entry
  - **Set location splash removed for returning users**: Removed the blocking "Please set your GPS location" splash branch; all post-first-login users auto-dismiss the splash; location manageable via My Locations in profile menu
  - **Map zoom buttons moved to bottom-right**: Changed Leaflet zoom control from `position:'topright'` to `position:'bottomright'` — no longer overlaps profile avatar menu
  - **Inbox badge clears on open**: Badge set to hidden immediately when inbox modal opens (optimistic clear); `apiPut('/api/messages/mark-all-read', {})` called after render to persist read status
  - **Backend `PUT /api/messages/mark-all-read`**: New endpoint in `src/routes/messages.js`; single DB UPDATE sets `is_read=true` for all unread messages where current user is receiver
- ✅ **Session 12 — UAT Phase 3 (commits `29c80e1`→`3cdfa22` on `uat`):**
  - **Listing detail Block A redesign (per UAT PDF)**: removed 140px `ld-img` block; `ld-info-bar` expanded to column layout; large 64px emoji icon always shown (no photo thumbnail); photo thumbnails strip `ld-photo-strip` at bottom of Block A; teal gradient distance bar `ld-dist-bar` at very top of Block A; price font changed to JetBrains Mono; "Mapit User" seller row removed from `ld-body`
  - **Category icon root cause fixed**: `listings_within_radius` Postgres RPC was not returning `subcategory` column → all listings fell back to generic 🚗; replaced RPC with JS haversine `SELECT *` in `listings.js` so all DB columns always returned
  - **Photos in detail view fixed**: list API only returns `cover_photo`, not `photos[]`; `selListing()` made async — renders immediately then fetches `/api/listings/:id` to populate `photos[]` and re-renders
  - **Subcategory form fix**: `updatePostSubcats()` now adds "— Select type —" disabled placeholder as first option so 'Cars' is never silently default; `submitListing()` validates subcategory is selected
  - **My Ads tab overhaul**: "📋 Displaying only your ads" header; ✏️ Edit + ✅ Sold buttons per card; clicking a listing keeps mine tab (no tab switch); detail view shows "Edit Listing" instead of Chat/Save; "Other nearby listings" suppressed entirely; `editListing(id)` pre-fills post form; `submitListing()` sends `PUT /api/listings/:id` in edit mode and resets `status='pending'`; backend PUT expanded to allow `category`, `subcategory`, `details`, `lat`, `lng`, `address`
  - **Supabase data fix needed**: existing TVS SCOOTY listing likely has `subcategory='Cars'` — user needs to run `UPDATE listings SET subcategory='2 Wheelers' WHERE title ILIKE '%TVS%'` in Supabase SQL Editor
- ✅ **Session 11 — UAT bug fixes + listing detail redesign (commits `13feb2f`, `735db81`, `8a6e494`):**
  - **CORS fix**: Vercel preview URLs (`mapit-backend-*.vercel.app`) now allowed in CORS regex — was blocking OTP email step on UAT; applied to `main` + merged into `uat` (commit `13feb2f`)
  - **Listing detail redesign (per PDF)**: removed standalone orange distance pill and duplicate title/price section; removed "Verified" badge and star rating from seller card; added compact `.ld-info-bar` below image with thumbnail | title + price | 📷 photo count | 💬 Chat with Seller | ♡ save | ⤴ share; image height increased to 140px; seller shown as simple name only
  - **Title placeholder per subcategory**: `TITLE_PLACEHOLDER` JS object maps category+subcategory to realistic example text; updates dynamically when form category/subcategory changes
  - **Manual GPS location for posting**: location dropdown always shows "📍 Enter GPS coordinates manually…" option at bottom; auto-selected when no saved pins; shows coord + label inputs; `submitListing()` validates and parses manual coords; Google Maps tip shown
- ✅ **Session 10 — Arun review: major UX + feature overhaul (on `dev`/`uat`, commit `27393b7`):**
  - **Font/readability**: base font 15px, form labels 12px, inputs 14px
  - **Category restructure**: Real Estate (Plots/Individual House/Builder Flat/Apartment/Commercial Building), Vehicles (Cars/2 Wheelers/Cycles/Trucks & Commercial), Household Items (Electronic Appliances/Furniture/Home Decor)
  - **Dynamic listing form**: `LISTING_FIELDS` data structure; fields render dynamically per category+subcategory per PDF spec
  - **Photo gallery viewer**: tap listing image to open full-screen viewer; ‹ › arrows + dot nav + counter
  - **My Locations**: radio (Use My Current Location / Enter GPS Coordinates) + single "Save Location" button
  - **Backend**: `details` JSONB stored; `hh` added to `VALID_CATEGORIES`; `condition` field removed; `price` derived from `details.total_price`

  - `API_URL` now auto-detects environment via `window.location.hostname`; UAT branch wired to Vercel preview
  - All 12 family invite codes entered in Supabase `invite_codes` table
