**📋 SESSION 8 — Admin Dashboard + Performance + UX Polish**
> Original scope: unplanned placeholder; anchored to admin tooling, performance audit, and UX polish (Testers 4, 5).
> 🆕 **Scope substantially expanded 2026-07-20 (Nagesh)**, ahead of starting the build. Context that drove the expansion: the admin page will be **mainly operated by Arun from Bangalore**, with Nagesh only occasionally involved (sometimes remotely from UAE) — and **Arun should be treated as a technical novice**. Before building, worked through the top 10 challenges MapIt is likely to face post-MVP-release and mapped each to a concrete admin-page capability; two of those (chat visibility on a report, suspend enforcement) turned out to be real functional gaps, not just UI consolidation, so they're now explicit build items rather than assumed-covered by "the admin page."

1. **Proper admin dashboard page (`/admin` route, real server-side admin-role gate)** — replaces the hardcoded-email-per-modal pattern with one Express middleware guarding the whole page; fixes the known gap that admin modals today are `requireAuth`-only, not admin-role-gated.
   - **Design principle for the whole page, not just this item:** novice-first. Plain language throughout (no "RLS", "JWT", raw IDs where a name will do), icons over jargon, confirm dialogs that state the consequence in one plain sentence ("This will permanently delete this listing and its 3 photos — cannot be undone"), mobile-usable (Arun/Nagesh may check it from a phone while traveling).
   - **Migrate, don't duplicate, both existing admin modals into this page, then remove them from the profile dropdown** (Nagesh's explicit instruction, 2026-07-20):
     - "🔐 Review Listings" modal → Pending Listings tab + Reported Listings tab
     - "💬 View Feedbacks" modal → Feedback Viewer tab
   - Dashboard summary tiles: listings by status (pending/active/expired/sold), new users this week, messages sent this week, open reports count, open grievances count.
   - **New — plain-language system health tile.** Pulls a simple issue count from Sentry (wired in Session 7) and shows "All systems OK" / "N new issues today" — Arun should never need to learn Sentry's own dashboard.
   - **New — in-page "What do I do?" playbook.** Short, plain-language reference mapping common situations (a listing gets reported, a user is abusive in chat, feedback needs a reply, an error alert email arrives) directly to the button/tab that handles it — replaces relying on Nagesh being reachable in the moment.
   - ~4–5 hrs (expanded from original ~3 hrs estimate).
2. ✅ **Suspend/Reinstate — real enforcement — CODE COMPLETE (2026-07-20), not yet UAT-tested.** `requireAuth` (`src/middleware/auth.js`) now checks `profiles.suspended` on every authenticated request (not just at login — an already-signed-in suspended user is rejected on their next call, not just kept out of future sign-ins), fail-open on a missing profile row so new-user registration is unaffected. Admin page's Pending/Reported/Feedback sections all got a shared Suspend/Reinstate toggle (`suspendBtnHtml()`/`toggleUserSuspension()`) reflecting live state, replacing the old one-way Suspend-only button. Main app's `api()` helper now forces a clean sign-out with one dedicated message on a `suspended:true` 403, instead of scattered generic error toasts. **Needs Nagesh's live click-test**: suspend a real test account → confirm it's rejected on its very next request (not just next login) → reinstate → confirm access returns immediately.
3. ✅ **Grievance log — UAT-PASSED (2026-07-20), committed.** New `grievances` table (`database/migrations/008-grievance-log.sql`, RLS enabled with **zero policies** — default-deny for anon/authenticated, only the backend's service-role client ever touches it, same treatment as `listing_reports`' no-SELECT-policy pattern), run by Nagesh in Supabase. New `src/routes/grievances.js` (list/create/acknowledge/resolve, all `requireAuth`+`requireAdmin`). Scope deliberately expanded past the original "date/complainant/note/deadline/status" description after flagging it to Nagesh first: IT Rules 2021 Rule 3(2) actually has **two** separate deadlines (acknowledge within 24h, resolve within 15 days), so added a nullable `acknowledged_at` column + a "Mark Acknowledged" button. Both deadlines computed on the fly from `received_at` (`grvBadges()`), never stored as columns. Admin page has a 4th block ("⚖️ Grievances") with a "+ Log New Grievance" form (name, optional contact, note, backdatable received-date — grievances arrive via Arun's personal email, no automatic capture) and deadline status shown as plain right-aligned colored text next to the name/date — iterated 3 times per Nagesh's feedback (pill badges → flat alert bars → plain text, plus repositioned to match a reference screenshot) since the pill/bar styling kept reading as clickable buttons. Full log→acknowledge→resolve flow click-tested and confirmed working by Nagesh.
4. **🆕 Reported-chat visibility.** When a listing or user is reported, let admin view the relevant conversation thread read-only from within the report — right now there's no way for Arun to actually see what was said in a reported chat before deciding to act. Privacy-conscious by design: only surfaced in the context of an actual report, never a general "browse all chats" capability. Needs a quick design check-in before building (exact trigger condition, what's shown) — flagged, not yet finalized. ~1.5–2 hrs.
5. **🆕 Bulk listing cleanup tool.** Multi-select stale/duplicate listings for removal in one action, instead of the current one-at-a-time flow — addresses map clutter as listing volume grows. ~1 hr.
6. **Performance audit — Lighthouse mobile** — run Lighthouse on `mapit.co.in` → Mobile → Performance; target First Contentful Paint <2s on simulated 4G; identify and fix top 2–3 opportunities (preload hints already done in Session 4; focus on image quality cap at upload, unused CSS). ~1 hr.
7. **`robots.txt` + `sitemap.xml`** — add `public/robots.txt` (Allow: /, Disallow: /api/, Sitemap: https://mapit.co.in/sitemap.xml) and `public/sitemap.xml` with root URL; add Express static route for `/sitemap.xml`. ~20 min.
8. **Marker clustering — Leaflet.markercluster (Tester-5)** — add `Leaflet.markercluster` CDN; wrap pin rendering in `L.markerClusterGroup()`; nearby pins merge into a numbered circle at low zoom levels, fan out on click. Addresses the scalability concern raised by Tester-5 for high-density listing scenarios (apartment complexes, bazaars at public-launch scale). Free, open source. ~2 hrs.
9. **Visual aesthetics UX polish pass (Tester-4a)** — select top 5 issues from `docs/ux-audit.md` with highest visible impact (visual hierarchy, spacing, typography weights, colour consistency); implement as a focused batch. Keep changes minimal and measurable. ~2 hrs.
10. ✅ **Admin dashboard metrics/analytics — UAT-PASSED (2026-07-20), committed.** Design pass done with Nagesh (active = posted-or-messaged in last 30d; region = curated Bangalore-locality text match against `address`; chart = hand-rolled SVG donut, no CDN dependency) after he shared a "StarAdmin" template for visual direction — decided to reimplement its structural ideas natively rather than import the template's own framework. Landed as a new "📊 Overview" block, now the admin page's default landing tab. New `src/routes/admin.js` (`GET /api/admin/overview`) + new `src/utils/bangaloreAreas.js` (also closes out the Session 04C "curated Bangalore-areas database" stretch goal). No new migration. Nagesh confirmed the numbers/chart look right against the dev DB and the other 4 blocks are unaffected.
11. 🆕 **"Explore Area" search bar (Nagesh, 2026-07-20, during item 2 UAT) — not started.** When "Explore Area" is toggled in the left panel, add a search bar (center-placed) to type a location and pan/zoom the map there — same pattern already built and shipped in Session 04C's post-listing/registration location pickers (Photon-based prefix search, `flyTo`-only, never auto-places a pin). Nagesh explicitly left the timing open ("later or now") — deferred per this session's own call to keep Session 8 item 2 (suspend enforcement) UAT focused; pick up as its own item, reusing `_onPostLocSearchInput()`/Photon-search plumbing rather than building fresh.

**Suggested build order (dependency-driven):** item 1 (the page + role-gate + migration) first since everything else lives inside it → item 2 (suspend enforcement, touches auth) → items 3–5 (new admin capabilities) → items 6–9 (independent, can interleave or defer).

**📋 SESSION 9 — Bundled Subscription Upgrades + Public Launch Gate**
> 🆕 **Reordered 2026-07-19 (Nagesh):** all three paid subscriptions (Vercel Pro, Supabase Pro, Resend Starter) now activate together here, as late as possible, instead of being spread across Sessions 7/9 — keeps recurring costs at $0 for as long as possible while all the free-tier-compatible work (Sentry, legal pages, CSP, admin dashboard, performance, PgBouncer prep) happens first.

**Bundled upgrade step (do first in this session):**
1. **Vercel Free → Pro upgrade** ($20/mo) — unlocks Vercel Cron Jobs (needed for the Session 5 expiry-notification cron) and branch-specific custom domains (`uat.mapit.co.in` will finally point to the UAT branch, not production). ~30 min.
2. **Supabase Free → Pro upgrade** ($25/mo) — unlocks daily automated backups (7-day retention), Point-in-Time Recovery (PITR), higher connection limits, Storage CDN, compute upgrade, no row-count limits. Before upgrading: run the SQL cleanup tasks below. ~30 min.
3. **Resend plan upgrade → Starter** ($20/mo) — free tier (100/day) is exhausted by ~50 daily active users once seller notifications + expiry emails + OTP emails are all live. Owner: Nagesh, 2 min in Resend dashboard.
4. **Vercel deployment/error-rate alert configuration** — set Vercel email alerts for failed deployments and function error-rate spikes (moved here from Session 7 since it's naturally done right alongside the Pro upgrade). ~15 min.

**Now unblocked by the upgrade above:**
5. **Wire the listing-expiry-notification cron** — the route was already built in Session 5; this step just wires the actual Vercel Cron Job trigger now that Cron Jobs are available.
6. **Supabase PgBouncer connection pooler** (moved from Session 8) — swap direct DB connection string for the Supabase pooler string in `.env` and Vercel env vars; prevents connection exhaustion at 50+ concurrent users (Supabase Free = 20 direct connections limit). One-line config change. ~20 min.
7. **Enable Supabase Storage CDN** — toggle CDN on in Supabase Dashboard → Storage → CDN; also enable Image Transformations for thumbnail-quality photos (reduces bandwidth ~60%). Dashboard toggle, not code. ~10 min.
8. **Verify backup and PITR configuration** — confirm daily backups scheduled in Supabase → Settings → Backups; do a test restore of one table to a temporary DB to confirm restorability — the one disaster-recovery check always skipped until it is needed. ~30 min.

**Launch gate:**
9. 🔴 **Pre-launch security and readiness checklist** — final automated check: (1) CORS allows only production + UAT, no localhost; (2) Helmet CSP active in response headers; (3) RLS enabled on all tables; (4) Sentry receiving test errors; (5) legal pages live at `/privacy` and `/terms`; (6) email verification flow works end-to-end; (7) expiry notification cron firing correctly; (8) Google OAuth redirect URLs in Supabase; (9) `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` absent from all client-visible responses. All 9 must pass before public launch. ~1.5 hrs.
10. **Soft-open (48 hrs before public announcement)** — share the live URL with 10–20 people outside family test group; monitor Sentry for new error types, Vercel logs for unexpected patterns; proceed with public announcement only after soft-open is clean.

- **Auth flow (new MVP):** email+password OR Google OAuth OR email OTP → Bearer token in ST.session → `requireAuth` middleware validates JWT; profile setup + home location on first login
- **Auth flow (family legacy):** existing family sessions restored from `mapit_sessions` localStorage; session expiry shows `step-auth` with email pre-filled
- **CORS:** allows `mapit.co.in`, `www.mapit.co.in`, `uat.mapit.co.in`, `localhost:3000`, `localhost:3001`, `localhost:5500`, `127.0.0.1:5500`, `mapit-backend-*.vercel.app` (case-insensitive regex). Localhost origins to be removed in Session 4 before public launch.
- **OTP rate limit (Express):** `OTP_RATE_LIMIT=100` per hour per IP (Vercel env var)
- **Resend free tier:** 100 emails/day — sufficient for family beta
- **`MapIt_MVP_v1.html`** is the canonical MVP frontend (2603 lines, single-file). `public/index.html` is the deployed copy — **always sync both after any frontend change** with `cp MapIt_MVP_v1.html public/index.html && diff MapIt_MVP_v1.html public/index.html`. `MapIt_Demo 30052026.html` is the prototype reference — do NOT edit it.
- **Two-file rule:** `MapIt_MVP_v1.html` ↔ `public/index.html` must always be byte-identical. Diff must show NOTHING before any commit.
- **Vercel `vercel.json` behaviour:** Root `vercel.json` is always read regardless of "Root Directory" project setting. Frontend served via `express.static` in `src/server.js`.
- **Helmet CSP:** Disabled globally (`contentSecurityPolicy: false`) — required for inline scripts and CDN resources. Planned re-enablement in Session 7 once `step-verify-email` (Session 4) reduces inline scripts.
- **Multi-session storage key:** `localStorage['mapit_sessions']` — JSON object keyed by Supabase user ID
- **Messages API:** `GET /api/messages/inbox`; `POST /api/messages`; `PUT /api/messages/mark-all-read`
- **Admin menu CSS:** `.uav-item.hide{display:none;}` is the critical rule
- **Branch strategy:** `dev` (local only) → `uat` (Vercel preview, family sign-off) → `main` (production); `API_URL` auto-detects
- **UAT URL:** `https://mapit-backend-git-uat-nagesh-n-arun.vercel.app` — Vercel Deployment Protection disabled for preview
- **Photo limit:** 5 per listing — enforced in both frontend (slice) and backend (multer + DB check)
- **`listings.details` JSONB**: stores all category-specific fields; `total_price` inside details is used as the canonical price
- **Category IDs:** `re` = Real Estate, `veh` = Vehicles, `hh` = Household Items; `furn` kept in VALID_CATEGORIES for legacy data only
- **`LISTING_FIELDS` JS object**: canonical field definitions per cat+subcat; drives form, hover card, and detail panel; vehicle subcategories now have `placeholder` for Brand/Model and `default:1` for owners field
- **`ST.favListings[]`**: separate from `ST.listings` — populated by `renderFavs()` via `GET /api/listings/saved/all`; used by `drawMarkers()` when `ST.tab==='fav'`; `fitBounds(padding:[50,50],maxZoom:14)` auto-fits map to all saved pins on Saved tab open; `viewFav()` uses `keepTab=true` so detail stays on Saved tab
- **Proximity radius (`.rad-box`)**: disabled (opacity .4, pointer-events:none) when `ST.tab !== 'browse'`; toggled by `switchTab()` and `selListing(!keepTab)`
- **Listing detail Block A layout (Session 12):** `ld-info-bar` is `flex-direction:column`; first child is `ld-dist-bar` (full-bleed teal gradient); second is `ld-info-top`; third is `ld-photo-strip`
- **`selListing(id, keepTab=false)`:** second param prevents tab switch when called from My Ads; `ST.editingId` tracks edit mode
- **Listings API list endpoint:** uses `SELECT *` + JS haversineM() filter; returns `distance_m` field; frontend maps `distance_m → d` (km)
- **Proximity buttons:** 3 km · 5 km · 10 km · City (999 km / City-wide) · Region (50 km); default 5 km
- **City map zoom:** `fitBounds` with 25 km box from user's active pin (not fixed zoom 12); Region uses `radCircle.getBounds()` fitBounds
- **SUBCAT_MAP vs CATS.subs:** `SUBCAT_MAP` (for post form) has no 'All Types'; `CATS[].subs` (for filter bar) includes 'All Types' — intentionally different
